Owner: Group Sustainability Reporting. Approved by the Sustainability Steering Committee on 2026-06-18.\\nIn force from 2026-07-01 for every Scope 2 inventory struck on or after that date (FY2026 onwards). Supersedes Revision 3\\n(2025-03-04). Word or PDF copies outside this space are uncontrolled; the April 2026 consultation draft is withdrawn.</p>\\n\\n<h2>1 Scope</h2>\\n<p>1.1 The inventory covers purchased electricity, purchased district steam and purchased chilled water of Talvik\\nPrecision Components Inc. and the subsidiaries in Schedule C, under operational control, for the 22 sites of the site\\nregister.</p>\\n<p>1.2 The fiscal year runs 1 October to 30 September. FY2026 is 2025-10-01 to 2026-09-30.</p>\\n<p>1.3 Every site reports a location-based and a market-based figure in t CO2e.</p>\\n\\n<h2>2 Records of the inventory and the strike date</h2>\\n<p>2.1 The inventory board (monday.com, one item per site) and the summary page in this space are the records of the\\ninventory.</p>\\n<p>2.2 The inventory is struck when the summary page is published. Activity data, certificate status and factors are\\ntaken as the records of reference stand at that moment; a change that reaches a record of reference before the strike\\nis part of the inventory.</p>\\n\\n<h2>3 Emission factors</h2>\\n<p>3.1 Factors come from the Talvik Emission Factor Set (EFS) edition and revision that Schedule A names for the\\ninventory year. A revision includes every erratum issued for it; errata are published as child pages of the EFS page in\\nthis space and are not merged into the distributed CSV, so the CSV of an edition is read together with its errata.</p>\\n<p>3.2 An edition published during or after the fiscal year does not apply to that year unless Schedule A says so.</p>\\n<p>3.3 The location factor is the EFS \\\"location\\\" row of the site's EFS region (site register); the residual mix is the\\n\\\"residual_mix\\\" row of the same region.</p>\\n\\n<h2>4 Activity data — purchased electricity</h2>\\n<p>4.1 The source of record is the supplier invoice, as filed by Facilities in the Box folder \\\"Facilities — Utility\\ninvoices FY2026\\\" (the invoice ledger and any reissued invoice filed beside it). Exports of the ledger kept elsewhere are\\nworking copies.</p>\\n<p>4.2 Where an invoice and a site meter disagree, the invoice governs. The desk raises the discrepancy with the\\nsupplier; the invoice stands as billed until the supplier reissues it.</p>\\n<p>4.3 Exception for estimated invoices: an invoice month with read type E is replaced by the metered consumption of the\\nsame period from the energy platform, i.e. the difference between the main meter's month-end register readings that\\nbound the billing period.</p>\\n<p>4.4 A reissued invoice replaces the invoice it corrects for the whole of its billing period from the moment it is\\nfiled in the invoice folder.</p>\\n<p>4.5 Quantities are taken in the unit the supplier bills; the ledger data dictionary records the accounts not billed in\\nkWh. Convert to MWh before applying factors.</p>\\n\\n<h2>5 Activity data — thermal carriers</h2>\\n<p>5.1 Steam quantities are the pounds (lb) on the supplier's monthly statement lines; chilled water quantities are the\\nton-hours on the supplier's monthly statement lines.</p>\\n<p>5.2 Steam energy: MMBtu = lb × enthalpy (Btu/lb) ÷ 1,000,000.</p>\\n<p>5.3 Enthalpy: where the site returns condensate to the supplier and the supplier credits it (site register\\n\\\"condensate_return\\\" = Yes), use the supplier's net delivered enthalpy; otherwise use the total enthalpy of the delivered\\nsteam. Both come from the supplier's enthalpy note for the period.</p>\\n<p>5.4 Steam factors: the location-based figure uses the EFS district-steam default of the revision in force. The\\nmarket-based figure uses the supplier's plant-specific factor only where that factor has been verified by an\\nindependent third party; an unverified, preliminary or indicative supplier factor is not used and the EFS default\\napplies.</p>\\n<p>5.5 Chilled water: kWh-equivalent = ton-hours × 12,000 ÷ 3,412.14 ÷ COP, where COP is the plant's measured annual\\ncoefficient of performance for the fiscal year as stated in the supplier's annual statement. Design, nameplate or\\n\\\"up to\\\" efficiencies are not a measured COP. Where the statement gives no measured COP, use the Schedule B default.</p>\\n<p>5.6 Chilled water takes the location factor and the residual mix of the site's EFS region. Contractual instruments\\nare not applied to chilled water.</p>\\n\\n<h2>6 Contractual instruments — quality criteria for the market-based figure</h2>\\n<p>An instrument reduces a site's market-based electricity only if it satisfies every clause below. The registry status\\nis read in the registry account mirror (Airtable), which Greenledger syncs from the registries.</p>\\n<p>6.1 Instruments apply to purchased electricity only.</p>\\n<p>6.2 Vintage: the generation period on the certificate lies wholly within 1 April 2025 to 31 December 2026 (six months\\nbefore the fiscal year to three months after it).</p>\\n<p>6.3 Market boundary: the certificate is issued in the market where the site consumes. US sites: NRR RECs. Sites in\\nGermany, the Netherlands and Poland: EECS Guarantees of Origin issued through AIB member registries. Sites in Great\\nBritain: Ofgem REGOs only; Guarantees of Origin are not accepted for GB consumption.</p>\\n<p>6.4 Retirement: the registry shows the certificate as Retired at the strike date. Active, instructed, scheduled,\\npending or reversed retirements do not qualify.</p>\\n<p>6.5 Beneficiary: the certificate is retired on behalf of Talvik Precision Components Inc. or one of the subsidiaries\\nin Schedule C. Retirements for any other party, including former group companies, do not qualify.</p>\\n<p>6.6 No double claim: a certificate claimed in any earlier inventory (a claim period of an earlier fiscal year, or a\\nlisting in that year's final inventory) is not claimed again.</p>\\n<p>6.7 Generator age: the generating facility was commissioned on or after 1 January 2011.</p>\\n<p>6.8 Power purchase agreements count only through the certificates they convey to Talvik, and those certificates must\\nthemselves meet 6.2 to 6.7. A contract that settles financially without conveying the attributes to Talvik is not an\\ninstrument, whatever volume it names.</p>\\n<p>6.9 Site and volume: an instrument covers only the site named in its registry retirement record (\\\"Site claimed\\\"), up\\nto that site's purchased electricity for the year. Volume above that is not carried to another site or to another year.</p>\\n<p>6.10 Every excluded instrument is recorded with the clause it fails.</p>\\n\\n<h2>7 Calculation</h2>\\n<p>7.1 Location-based t CO2e = (electricity MWh + chilled-water MWh-equivalent) × location factor + steam MMBtu × EFS\\ndistrict-steam default ÷ 1,000.</p>\\n<p>7.2 Market-based t CO2e = (electricity MWh − MWh covered by qualifying instruments) × residual mix + chilled-water\\nMWh-equivalent × residual mix + steam MMBtu × market-based steam factor ÷ 1,000.</p>\\n<p>7.3 Where the EFS revision in force publishes no residual-mix factor for a region, the location factor of that region\\nis used in its place.</p>\\n<p>7.4 Carry full precision through the calculation and round each site figure to one decimal place at the end. Totals\\nare the sum of the rounded site figures as entered on the board.</p>\\n\\n<h2>8 Reporting</h2>\\n<p>8.1 On the board, each site item carries \\\"Location-based tCO2e\\\" and \\\"Market-based tCO2e\\\" as numbers to one decimal\\nplace, and \\\"Instrument applied\\\": the registry Certificate ID of every qualifying instrument for the site, in ascending\\norder, separated by a semicolon and a space; or the words <code>Residual mix</code> where no instrument qualifies.</p>\\n<p>8.2 The summary page states the two totals and lists every excluded instrument by its registry Certificate ID, or by\\nits contract reference where no certificate was conveyed.</p>\\n\\n<h2>Schedule A — EFS edition and revision by inventory year</h2>\\n<table>\\n<tr><th>Inventory year</th><th>EFS edition</th><th>Revision in force</th><th>Note</th></tr>\\n<tr><td>FY2024</td><td>EFS-2023</td><td>2023.3</td><td></td></tr>\\n<tr><td>FY2025</td><td>EFS-2024</td><td>2024.1</td><td></td></tr>\\n<tr><td>FY2026</td><td>EFS-2025</td><td>2025.2 (Erratum 2 of 2026-02-17)</td><td>EFS-2026 was published 2026-08-14, after the start of FY2026; it is not used for FY2026.</td></tr>\\n<tr><td>FY2027</td><td>EFS-2026</td><td>2026.1</td><td></td></tr>\\n</table>\\n\\n<h2>Schedule B — constants</h2>\\n<p>12,000 Btu per ton-hour; 3,412.14 Btu per kWh; default chilled-water plant COP 4.0; 1 MWh = 1,000 kWh; 1 t = 1,000 kg.</p>\\n\\n<h2>Schedule C — subsidiaries in the organisational boundary (FY2026)</h2>\\n<p>Talvik Precision Components GmbH (DE); Talvik Precision Components B.V. (NL); Talvik Precision Components Sp. z o.o.\\n(PL); Talvik Precision Components Ltd (GB). {"type": "user", "message": {"role": "user", "content": [{"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": "toolu_reference_0005", "is_error": false, "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "# Utility invoice ledger — data dictionary (Facilities shared services)\n\nOwner: Facilities Shared Services, Accounts Payable interface team. Version 7, 2026-04-02.\nApplies to: the FY2026 electricity invoice ledger filed in Box (\"Facilities — Utility invoices FY2026\") and every CSV\nexport taken from it (the monthly exports the sustainability desk keeps are working copies of the same table).\n\n| # | Field | Type | Meaning |\n|---|-------|------|---------|\n| 1 | site_code | text | Talvik site code, TPC-01 … TPC-22 (site register) |\n| 2 | account_no | text | Supplier account number as printed on the invoice |\n| 3 | supplier | text | Supplier trading name |\n| 4 | invoice_no | text | Supplier invoice number; a reissued invoice carries the prefix of the supplier followed by RE |\n| 5 | period_start / period_end | date | Billing period, inclusive, ISO 8601 |\n| 6 | read_type | code | A = actual meter read by the supplier; E = estimated by the supplier; C = customer read (not used in FY2026) |\n| 7 | consumption_kwh | number | Billed active energy for the period, as billed (see note 3) |\n| 8 | amount | number | Invoice total excluding VAT/sales tax, in `currency` |\n| 9 | currency | ISO 4217 | Invoice currency |\n| 10 | posted_on | date | Date AP posted the invoice to the ledger |\n\nNotes\n\n1. One row per supplier invoice. Credit notes are not carried in this ledger; a supplier correction arrives as a\n   reissued invoice and is filed next to the ledger in the same Box folder, never as an edit of the original row.\n2. Amounts are not converted to USD. Do not use `amount` for any consumption estimate.\n3. Unit of `consumption_kwh`: the loader copies the billed quantity unchanged. Every account is billed in kWh\n   except account BGS-PL-40917 (Baltic Grid Supply, Gdańsk), whose supplier bills in MWh to one decimal place; for\n   that account the column holds MWh as billed. The column was not renamed because the AP interface schema is shared\n   with 41 other entities (change request FSS-CR-2291 declined 2025-11-14).\n4. Odra Energia (account OEN-PL-11930, Wrocław) moved to kWh billing in 2024; its rows are kWh like every other account.\n5. Estimated invoices (`read_type` = E) are trued up by the supplier on a later actual read for some suppliers only;\n   in FY2026 none of the five E invoices was trued up in the ledger.\n6. `posted_on` is the AP posting date, not the supplier's issue date.\n"}]}]}}
